export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_food_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_food_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
if = {
 limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_cost_food value > 0 } check_variable = { which = aqm_factor value < 0.1 } }
 remove_country_flag = aqm_price_supported
}
multiply_variable = { which = aqm_cost_food value = aqm_factor }
set_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = 1 }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_energy_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_energy_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
if = {
 limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_cost_energy value > 0 } check_variable = { which = aqm_factor value < 0.1 } }
 remove_country_flag = aqm_price_supported
}
multiply_variable = { which = aqm_cost_energy value = aqm_factor }
set_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = 1 }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_minerals_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }

export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_minerals_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
if = {
 limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_cost_minerals value > 0 } check_variable = { which = aqm_factor value < 0.1 } }
 remove_country_flag = aqm_price_supported
}
multiply_variable = { which = aqm_cost_minerals value = aqm_factor }
set_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = 1 }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = country_minor_artifacts_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
export_modifier_to_variable = { modifier = megastructures_minor_artifacts_cost_mult variable = aqm_modifier }
change_variable = { which = aqm_factor value = aqm_modifier }
if = {
 limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_cost_minor_artifacts value > 0 } check_variable = { which = aqm_factor value < 0.1 } }
 remove_country_flag = aqm_price_supported
}
multiply_variable = { which = aqm_cost_minor_artifacts value = aqm_factor }

if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = aqm_price_supported } } change_variable = { which = aqm_price_skipped value = 1 }
 }
 }
if = { limit = { root = { has_country_flag = aqm_price_supported
check_variable = { which = aqm_used value < aqm_cap }
check_variable = { which = aqm_budget_alloys value >= aqm_cost_alloys }
check_variable = { which = aqm_budget_unity value >= aqm_cost_unity }
check_variable = { which = aqm_budget_food value >= aqm_cost_food }
check_variable = { which = aqm_budget_energy value >= aqm_cost_energy }
check_variable = { which = aqm_budget_minerals value >= aqm_cost_minerals }
check_variable = { which = aqm_budget_minor_artifacts value >= aqm_cost_minor_artifacts }
}
 }
if = { limit = { root = { has_country_flag = aqm_execute } }
root = { export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = alloys variable = aqm_before_alloys }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = unity variable = aqm_before_unity }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = food variable = aqm_before_food }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = energy variable = aqm_before_energy }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = minerals variable = aqm_before_minerals }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = minor_artifacts variable = aqm_before_minor_artifacts }
 }
upgrade_megastructure_to = ring_world_3_intermediate
if = { limit = { is_upgrading = yes } root = { export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = alloys variable = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_before_alloys }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_cost_alloys }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_paid }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_due value > 0 } }
add_resource = { alloys = -1 mult = aqm_due }
}
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = unity variable = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_before_unity }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_cost_unity }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_paid }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_due value > 0 } }
add_resource = { unity = -1 mult = aqm_due }
}
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = food variable = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_before_food }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_cost_food }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_paid }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_due value > 0 } }
add_resource = { food = -1 mult = aqm_due }
}
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = energy variable = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_before_energy }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_cost_energy }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_paid }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_due value > 0 } }
add_resource = { energy = -1 mult = aqm_due }
}
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = minerals variable = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_before_minerals }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_cost_minerals }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_paid }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_due value > 0 } }
add_resource = { minerals = -1 mult = aqm_due }
}
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = minor_artifacts variable = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_before_minor_artifacts }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_paid value = aqm_after }
set_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_cost_minor_artifacts }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_due value = aqm_paid }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { which = aqm_due value > 0 } }
add_resource = { minor_artifacts = -1 mult = aqm_due }
}
change_variable = { which = aqm_ready value = 1 }
change_variable = { which = aqm_used value = 1 }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_alloys value = aqm_cost_alloys }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_alloys value = aqm_cost_alloys }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_unity value = aqm_cost_unity }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_unity value = aqm_cost_unity }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_food value = aqm_cost_food }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_food value = aqm_cost_food }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_energy value = aqm_cost_energy }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_energy value = aqm_cost_energy }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_minerals value = aqm_cost_minerals }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_minerals value = aqm_cost_minerals }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_minor_artifacts value = aqm_cost_minor_artifacts }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_minor_artifacts value = aqm_cost_minor_artifacts }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = alloys variable = aqm_budget_alloys }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = unity variable = aqm_budget_unity }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = food variable = aqm_budget_food }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = energy variable = aqm_budget_energy }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = minerals variable = aqm_budget_minerals }
export_resource_stockpile_to_variable = { resource = minor_artifacts variable = aqm_budget_minor_artifacts }
 } }
else = { root = { change_variable = { which = aqm_failed value = 1 }
 } }
}
else = { root = { change_variable = { which = aqm_ready value = 1 }
change_variable = { which = aqm_used value = 1 }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_alloys value = aqm_cost_alloys }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_alloys value = aqm_cost_alloys }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_unity value = aqm_cost_unity }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_unity value = aqm_cost_unity }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_food value = aqm_cost_food }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_food value = aqm_cost_food }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_energy value = aqm_cost_energy }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_energy value = aqm_cost_energy }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_minerals value = aqm_cost_minerals }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_minerals value = aqm_cost_minerals }
subtract_variable = { which = aqm_budget_minor_artifacts value = aqm_cost_minor_artifacts }
change_variable = { which = aqm_total_minor_artifacts value = aqm_cost_minor_artifacts }
 } }
}
}
}
}
remove_country_flag = aqm_price_supported
remove_country_flag = aqm_execute
}
